Compute c via FFT:
```
def fft_conv(a):
    n=len(a); M=2*n-1; N=1<<(M-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
```
Potential numerical negative tiny; max c positive. For score, use max.

Compute LSE:
```
def lse_value_grad(a, beta, topk=None):
    n=len(a); c=fft_conv(a); S=a.sum()
    if topk:
       # need select top k coefficients by value. Use argsort O(MlogM) expensive but M~14k; okay not per eval? L-BFGS thousands => maybe heavy. Can approximate topk indices from previous? Simpler use plain LSE.
    cmax = c.max(); z=exp(beta*(c-cmax)); Z=z.sum(); L=cmax+log(Z)/beta; p=z/Z
    # D = R + a_i W_i
    ar=a[::-1]
    # conv ar and p length? p length M=2n-1. We need N2 next pow2 n+M-1=3n-2.
    N2=1<<((n+M-1)-1).bit_length()
    R=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ar,N2)*np.fft.rfft(p,N2),N2)[:n+M-1]
    R=R[n-1:2*n-1]
    # W_i = sum_{k=i}^{i+M-1? p length M=2n-1, i+n-1 inclusive: length n}
    # p indices 0..2n-2. For i=0, k 0..n-1. i=n-1, k=n-1..2n-2.
    cp = np.concatenate([0, np.cumsum(p)])
    W = cp[n:n+(?)]? cp[i+n]-cp[i-?]. For i, end index i+n-1 inclusive => prefix[end+1]=prefix[i+n]. start i => prefix[i]. W=cp[i+n]-cp[i]. length n. i=0 cp[n]-cp[0]; i=n-1 cp[2n-1]-cp[n-1]. Total p length 2n-1, cp length 2n. OK.
    D=R + a*W
    grad = alpha*(D/S^2 - 2*L/S^3)
    return alpha*L/S^2, grad
```

W_i: i=0 p0+p1=2/3, i=1 p1+p2=2/3. D=[(x+y)/3+(x+y)/3=2S/3, same]. Matches true uniform derivative. Good.

```
def softmax_lse_score_grad(y, beta, topk=None):
    p = softmax(y)
    S=1.0 (but due numerical exactly sum=1)
    c=conv(p,p)
    L,pw = lse(c,beta)
    # F = α L, no S factor if S=1.
    D = R + p*W (p_i W_i)
    g = α D
    gy = p * (g - np.sum(p*g))
    return α*L, gy
```

Detailed algorithm design:

```
def get_parent():
    try:
       p = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if p is not None:
          arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          arr = np.clip(arr,0,1000)
          if arr.size>=32 and arr.sum()>1e-8: return arr/arr.sum()
    except: pass
    return None
```

```
def lse_value_grad_log(y, beta):
    p = softmax_stable(y)
    c = conv_fft(p)
    S = p.sum() (1)
    cmax = c.max()
    z = np.exp(beta*(c-cmax))
    # if beta*cmax large but cmax shifted no
    Z = z.sum()
    if Z<=0: weights uniform?
    L = cmax + np.log(Z)/beta
    w = z/Z
    D = derivative_weights(p,w)
    g = (2*n) * D
    pg = np.dot(p,g)
    gy = p*(g-pg)
    val = (2*n)*L  # since S=1
    # maybe if not finite return large
    return float(val), gy
```
But if n large and beta large, `beta*(c-cmax)` <0; if beta=1e6, fine. If c-cmax around -1e-12, under exp no issue. For β infinite, weights=onehot. We can implement if beta > 1e12: use top1 active.

```
def derivative_weights(a, w):
    n=len(a); M=2*n-1
    # R via FFT
    ar = a[::-1]
    N=1 << (n+M-1 -1).bit_length()
    R = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ar,N)*np.fft.rfft(w,N), N)[:n+M-1]
    R = R[n-1:2*n-1]
    cp = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
    W = cp[n:2*n] - cp[0:n]? Need length n: cp[i+n]-cp[i]. For i 0..n-1. cp length 2n. `W = cp[n:2*n] - cp[0:n]`? For i=0: cp[n]-cp[0]; for i=n-1: cp[2n-1]-cp[n-1]. cp[n:2*n] indices n..2n-1, cp[0:n] 0..n-1. Good.
    return R + a*W
```
Check cp length: w length M=2n-1, cumsum length 2n-1, concat 0 length 2n. cp[2*n-1] = sum w; okay. For i=n-1, i+n=2n-1. Good.

def active_weights(c, top_frac=0.02, beta=0):
    M=len(c); T=int(min(M, max(1, top_frac*M)))
    if beta>0: weights softmax top? else uniform over top T.

```

```
def exact_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return inf
    c=conv_fft(a/S)  # normalized to S=1
    return 2*len(a)*float(np.max(c))
```

    n=a.size; R=zeros(n); W=zeros(n)
    for k,w in zip(idx,vals):
        w *=? already weight
        i_start=max(0,k-n+1); i_end=min(k,n-1)
        if i_end < i_start: continue
        idx=np.arange(i_start,i_end+1)
        # need a_{k-i}: as i increases, k-i decreases.
        # create slice j = k-i_end .. k-i_start? If a_j.
        j_start = k - i_end
        j_end = k - i_start

        W[i_start:i_end+1] += w
    return R + a*W
```

Time limit inside `run_lbfgs_log`:
```
def run_lbfgs_log(a0, beta, time_limit_s, tag=''):
    start=time.monotonic()
    n=len(a0)
    y0=np.log(a0 + 1e-300)
    y0 = y0 - np.mean(y0)
    maxfun = int(min(5000, max(50, time_limit_s * 80)))
    maxiter = max(50, maxfun * 2)
    callback = partial(lse_obj_log, beta=beta)
    try:
        res = minimize(callback, y0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                       options={'ftol':1e-16, 'gtol':1e-4, 'maxiter':maxiter, 'maxfun':maxfun, 'maxls':50})
    except Exception as e:
       print('LBFGS error', e); return a0, exact_score(a0)
    ...
```

Let's implement `smoothed_obj_y(y, beta)` returns val, grad with p computed and optionally floor:
```
def softmax_p(y):
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    y = y - np.max(y)
    y = np.clip(y, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <= 0: return ones/n
    p = e/s
    return p
```
If y all -700 due clip? max before clip maybe? We subtract max so max 0; exp(0)=1 => sum≥1. Good.

`lse_obj_y(y, beta)`:
- p=softmax_p(y)
- c=conv_real(p)
- S=p.sum() (1)
- cmax=np.max(c)
- if beta <=0: val=2n*cmax, weights uniform? For beta=0, derivative of average? Not needed. Use beta>0.
- diff = beta*(c-cmax); clip lower -745.
- z=np.exp(diff)
- Z=z.sum()
- if Z==0: z uniform? Actually cmax finite, exp(0)=1, Z>=1.
- L = cmax + log(Z)/beta
- w=z/Z
- D=derivative_weights(p,w) (FFT dense)
- g=2*n*D
- pg=np.dot(p,g)
- gy=p*(g-pg)
- val=(2*n)*L
- Replace nonfinite grad with 0.
- return val, gy

Maybe use `np.log(Z)`; Z maybe up to M. Good.

def mirror_step(a, beta_w=None, top_T=None, step=0.05):
   c=conv_real(a); M
   if top_T: idx=np.argpartition(c, -T); w sparse; D=derivative_weights_sparse
   else: weights softmax beta_w; D=derivative_weights
   g=alpha*d
   grad_y = a*(g - np.dot(a,g))
   y=np.log(a+1e-300); y_new=y - step*grad_y; a_new=softmax_p(y_new)
   score=exact_score(a_new)
   return a_new, score
```

Let's implement `lse_obj_a(a, beta, lam=100.0)` for L-BFGS-B:
```
def lse_obj_a(a, beta):
    a = np.maximum(a, 0)
    n=len(a); S=a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12: return 1e10, zeros
    c=conv_real(a)
    cmax=c.max(); z=exp(beta*(c-cmax)); Z; L=cmax+logZ/beta
    score=2n*L/(S*S)
    w=z/Z
    D=derivative_weights(a,w)  # uses a unnormalized
    g=2n*(D/(S*S) - 2*L/(S*S*S))
    g += 2*lam*(S-1.0)  # penalty gradient
    # maybe clip g to finite
    val=score + lam*(S-1.0)**2
    return val, g
```
This uses derivative_weights with a not normalized and w sum 1. D for c derivative wrt a. Good. Check if c and D scale: if a scaled t, c t^2, D 2t? Wait c derivative wrt a scales t, D linear in a; score α D/S^2 invariant (t cancels). Penalty gradient 2λ(S-1) scales with S. Good.

Bounds: `[(0, upper)]*n`, upper maybe 10? Since normalized sum 1, no value >10. But during optimization might try >10? Set upper=10.0 or 100? The evaluator caps at 1000, but for n=7000 sum 1, upper 10 too large. Bounds [0, 10] maybe fine. L-BFGS with 7000 bounds okay. But if a_i could need be O(1/n^0.5) ~0.01? power α=0.49 a0~0.017; <10. Uniform upper 10. Good.

This avoids log underflow and gradient small. L-BFGS-B with many bounds may be okay. We can use it for main optimization.

Can we formulate A matrix efficiently? A_{k,i}= f_{?}. For convolution f*g coefficient k =∑_i f_i g_{k-i} (bounds). For fixed f, A is a banded Toeplitz (subdiagonal) of size M x n. For m=512, dense okay (1023x512 ≈524k floats 4MB). For m=1024 ~2M 16MB, linprog maybe slow but possible. For m=2048 ~8M 64MB, maybe too slow. Use m=512 or 768.

Let's implement LP operator for coarse m (<=768):
```
def lp_update(f, M=None, solver='highs'):
    m=len(f); M = max(f*f) if None
    A = sparse.lil_matrix((2m-1,m))
    for k in range(2m-1):
       i_start=max(0,k-m+1); i_end=min(k,m-1)
       for i in range(i_start,i_end+1): A[k,i]=f[i]? Wait c_k=∑_{i+j=k} f_i g_j. For fixed k,j, i=k-j. So A[k,j]=f_{k-j} if valid. So for j, i=k-j. We can fill loop j.
    for j in range(m):
       k_start=max(0,j?); Actually i valid 0..m-1, k=i+j. For each i in valid, k=i+j, A[k,j]=f[i].
    # constraints A@g <= M
    res=linprog(-ones, A_ub=A, b_ub=M*ones, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
    g=res.x; if res.status not 0/4? 
    return g/g.sum()
```
This is O(m^2) fill; m=768 okay. Need import scipy.sparse? Allowed? Scientific libraries likely. But rule says scipy, numpy, cvxpy... not explicitly sparse but scipy.sparse part of scipy. Should be okay. To be safe, use dense for m<=512? Duplicates. Could use `scipy.sparse.lil_matrix`.

`resample(a, n)`: top-level.
```
def resample(a,n):
    if len(a)==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a); b=np.clip(b,0,1000); s=b.sum(); if s<1e-12: b=np.ones(n)*0.5; else b=b/s; return b
```

def lp_conv_update(f, M=None):
    f=np.asarray(f); m=len(f); f=f/sum
    if M is None: M=np.max(conv_real(f)) * 1.0001 + 1e-15
    from scipy.sparse import lil_matrix
    A=lil_matrix((2*m-1,m), dtype=float)
    for i in range(m):
        fi=f[i]
        if fi==0: continue
        start=i; end=i+m
        # A[i:i+m, i]? For k=i..i+m-1, A[k,i]=f[i]? Since c_k=sum_i f_i g_{k-i}. For fixed i (f index), g index j=k-i ranges 0..m-1 -> k=i..i+m-1. So A[k,i]=f[i].
        A[i:i+m, i] = fi
    cst = A.toarray()? linprog can take sparse? scipy linprog accepts sparse for high? Yes.
    res=linprog(-np.ones(m), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*m-1,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*m, method='highs')
    if res.success or res.status in (0,4): g=res.x; g=clip(g,0); s=g.sum(); if s>0 return g/s
    return None
```

```

Need cross_corr function:
```
def cross_corr(a,w):
   n=len(a); M=len(w)
   ar=a[::-1]
   N=next_pow2(n+M-1)
   res=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ar,N)*np.fft.rfft(w,N),N)
   return res[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check orientation for D: R_i=∑_k w_k a_{k-i}. For center n=2, ar=[y,x], conv with w center gave [y,x]=R. D=2R. Good.

For sparse active derivative: D_i=2∑_{k∈active}w_k a_{k-i}. For fixed k, valid i range: i from max(0,k-n+1) to min(k,n-1); a_{k-i} as i increases => j=k-i decreases from k-i_start to k-i_end. `R[start:end+1] += w * a[j_start:j_end+1][::-1]`. Then D=2R.

For LSE dense derivative, just R FFT.

def perturb_sequence(a, rng, scale=0.03, mode=0):
    n=len(a); x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if mode==0: b=a*(1+scale*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode==1: freq=...; phase=...; b=a*(1+scale*np.sin(2πfreq x+phase))
    elif mode==2: asym = rng.standard_normal(n) - rng.standard_normal(n)[::-1]; b=a + 0.5*scale*asym
    elif mode==3: sel=random subset; b=a.copy(); b[sel]*=...
    elif mode==4: mix with power

LP iteration mixing:
```
def lp_refine(f, n_iter=2, mix=0.5, deadline=None):
   best=f
   for it in range(n_iter):
      if time>deadline break
      g=lp_update(f)
      if g is None: break
      f1=normalize((1-mix)*best + mix*g)
      sc=exact_score(f1)
      if sc<exact_score(best): best=f1
   return best
```

def make_initial_sequences(n, rng, parent=None, limit=6):
   out=[]
   def add(a): a=normalize(clip(a,0,1000)); if finite and sum>0: out.append(a.copy())
   if parent is not None: add(resample(parent,n))
   add(np.ones(n))
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   for alpha in [0.3,0.45]:
      a=np.exp(-alpha*np.log(np.clip(x,1e-9,None))); add(a)
      add(a[::-1])
   for lam in [-1.0,1.0]: add(np.exp(lam*(2*x-1)))
   add(np.maximum(0.1, 1.0 - 2*abs(x-0.5)))  # triangle decreasing? maybe
   add(np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.1)
   # mix power + uniform
   a=add? ...
   if limit: out=out[:limit]
   return out
```

`normalize` should add tiny floor? If a has all zeros after clip, return ones/n. But zeros valid; if mostly zero, okay. For optimization, all zeros invalid. Use:
```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-12:
       return np.ones(len(a), dtype=float)/len(a) if len(a)>0 else np.ones(1)
    return a/S
```
No floor; zeros allowed. But for LBFGS lower bound, zeros okay. For perturb log? no log.

Cross corr:
```
def cross_corr(a,w):
   n=len(a); M=len(w); L=n+M-1; N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
   ar=a[::-1]
   A=np.fft.rfft(ar,N); W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   return np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:2*n-1]  # slice n-1 to 2n-1 length n
```
If w length maybe 2n-1. Good. For LSE objective, w length 2n-1.

w=z/Z
L=cmax + np.log(Z)/beta
```

Another possible improvement: Since objective is max coefficient, at optimum many coefficients active. We can optimize a smooth surrogate that uses top K coefficients equally (not LSE) with L-BFGS. This avoids smoothing parameter and gives exact gradient for weighted sum of top K. We can maintain top K fixed? Coefficients shift. But we can at each run compute top K of current a and optimize weighted sum with fixed weights. L-BFGS gradient uses constant weights (updated once per run). This may reduce specific peaks. We can implement a function `weighted_obj_a(a, weights)` where weights fixed length M (top K uniform). L-BFGS minimizes that plus penalty. Then after run, update weights to new top K and repeat. This is like successive linear/quadratic (max as weighted sum). It could be better than LSE. For each run, pick K=20 maybe. The weighted sum objective is smooth quadratic with sparse weights; derivative D=2R with sparse weights. L-BFGS can minimize exactly. After convergence, max might be different; repeat. This avoids beta. We can use it for final polish.

This uses D sparse if weights sparse. Need `cross_corr_sparse` for top K. Implement efficient sparse:
```
def cross_corr_sparse_indices(a, idx, vals):
   n=len(a); R=np.zeros(n)
   for k, val in zip(idx, vals):
       if val==0: continue
       i_start=max(0,k-n+1); i_end=min(k,n-1)
       if i_end<i_start: continue
       j_start=k-i_end; j_end=k-i_start
       R[i_start:i_end+1] += val * a[j_start:j_end+1][::-1]
   return R
```
Then D=2R. For K=50, loop 50, okay.
Weights sum1 (vals=1/K). J=∑vals c[idx].
Penalty as before. Objective value αJ + λ(S-1)^2. Gradient formula works for general weighted sum J (not LSE): ∂J=D. F=αJ/S^2; gradient α/S(D-2J). Good.

This weighted sum of top peaks may avoid smoothing and directly lower active peaks. We can use as an alternative to LSE for high-beta. After optimizing top K, the max may be unchanged or another peak rises. Iterate with new top K. Also can use K varied [10,50,100]. This is like block coordinate subgradient but L-BFGS.

def local_optimize_a(a0, deadline, global_state, local_state, rng, tag):
   a=normalize(a0)
   update local/global sc0

      t_left=deadline-time-10
      # allocate equal
      alloc=min(120, t_left/ (remaining))

7. `def update_state(global_state, local_state, sc, a, tag):`
```
def update_state(global_state, local_state, sc, a, tag=''):
    sc=float(sc)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return False
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return False
    a=np.clip(np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0),0,1000)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return False
    if sc < local_state['score'] - 1e-13:
       local_state['score']=sc; local_state['a']=a/S
       print(f"  {tag}: {sc:.10f} n={a.size}")
       if sc < global_state['score'] - 1e-13:
           global_state['score']=sc; global_state['a']=local_state['a'].copy()
       return True
    return False
```
But if local_state has a different length, score comparable? Yes objective absolute. If same length, okay. If local_state used for length, don't update with other length. We'll pass local. Global score can improve with any length. Good.

Potential: If local_state is for coarse, and we use update_state with global_state, coarse scores comparable? They include length factor; higher n maybe lower, but okay. We might not want coarse to update global because final need high n? But any valid sequence, coarse length can be returned if best. Coarse length 512 likely score >1.5? Maybe above 1.5, not best. Fine. But if parent not available and high-dim fails, coarse could be returned. We can update global from coarse; it's valid. But if later high-dim better, global updates.

However local_state for main length should not update with coarse; we call update_state with separate local_state. Global can. In `run_lbfgs_a`, we pass both. If we use local_state for main and global, update only if better local. If a different length global but not local, no local.

8. `def get_parent_sequence():`
```
def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        p = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
        if p is None: return None
        arr=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
        if arr.size>=16 and arr.sum()>1e-8:
           return arr/arr.sum()
    except Exception: pass
    return None
```

9. `def resample_seq(a,n):`
```
def resample_seq(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=int(n)
    if n<=0: n=1
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if a.size==n: return normalize(a)
    xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size); xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    b=np.interp(xn,xo,a)
    return normalize(b)
```

10. `def make_initial_seqs(n, rng, parent=None, limit=8):` as above. Need no nested functions; can use manual add via list and `append`. We can define local? No nested. Use top-level and append.
```
def make_initial_seqs(n,rng,parent=None,limit=8):
    out=[]
    def? no nested. We'll repeat normalize.
    if parent is not None:
       b=resample_seq(parent,n)
       if b.size: out.append(b)
    out.append(normalize(np.ones(n)))
    x=(np.arange(n,dtype=float)+0.5)/n
    for alpha in (0.3,0.45):
       b=np.exp(-alpha*np.log(np.clip(x,1e-12,None)))
       out.append(normalize(b))
       b2=b[::-1].copy()
       out.append(normalize(b2))
    for lam in (-1.0,1.0):
       out.append(normalize(np.exp(lam*(2*x-1))))
    out.append(normalize(np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))+0.1))
    # window
    out.append(normalize(0.2+0.8*np.cos(np.pi*(x-0.5)**1? Use np.maximum(0.1, np.cos(np.pi*x))**2))
    if limit: out=out[:limit]
    # remove duplicates? not necessary
    return out
```
Need ensure no nested; fine.

11. `def perturb_seq(a, rng, scale=0.03, mode=0):` top-level.
```
mode=mode%5
n=len(a)
if mode==0: b=a*(1+scale*rng.standard_normal(n))
elif mode==1: freq=1+10*rng.random(); phase=2π rng.random(); b=a*(1+scale*np.sin(2πfreq*x+phase))
elif mode==2: r=rng.standard_normal(n); asym=r-r[::-1]; b=a+0.5*scale*asym
elif mode==3: sel=rng.choice(n, max(1,n//10), replace=False); b=a.copy(); b[sel]*=(1+2*scale*rng.random(len(sel))-scale)
else: b=a*(1+scale*(rng.random(n)-0.5)*2)
return normalize(np.clip(b,0,1000))
```
`rng.choice` with replace=False. For n small okay.

12. `def lse_obj_a(a, beta, lam=10.0):`
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
a=np.clip(np.nan_to_num(a,...),0,1000)
n=a.size; S=a.sum()
if S<1e-12:
   return 1e6, np.zeros(n)? gradient negative?
   return 1e6, -np.ones(n)
p=a/S
c=conv_real(p)
cmax=float(np.max(c))
if beta<=1e-8:
   w=np.full(c.size,1.0/c.size); L=float(np.mean(c))
else:
   diff=beta*(c-cmax)
   diff=np.clip(diff,-745,0)
   z=np.exp(diff); Z=z.sum()
   if Z<=0 or not finite: w uniform; L=cmax
   else: w=z/Z; L=cmax+np.log(Z)/beta
R=cross_corr(p,w)
D=2.0*R
alpha=2.0*n
g=(alpha/S)*(D - 2.0*L)
g=g+2.0*lam*(S-1.0)
val=alpha*L + lam*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
if not finite val: val=1e6; g=zeros
if not finite g all: g=zeros
return float(val), g
```
For S<1e-12, gradient should encourage positive values. Since objective val large, L-BFGS decreases variables if gradient negative? Function val 1e6, gradient -1 means decreasing variable increases val? Wait minimize: gradient negative means f decreases by increasing variable? Gradient sign: if gradient negative, moving +x decreases f (since df=g dx). We want move from zero to positive to reduce large val; gradient should be negative. So return -np.ones(n) good. Bounds lower 0.

But if a has some zeros and S positive, gradient may be positive at zeros causing L-BFGS decrease below zero? Bound stops. okay.

15. `def run_lbfgs_lse(a0, beta, time_limit_s, global_state, local_state, tag):`
```
def _lbfgs(a0,beta,time_limit,global_state,local_state,tag,object_func, name):
   start=time.monotonic()
   a0=normalize(a0)
   sc0=exact_score(a0)
   update_state(global_state,local_state,sc0,a0,tag+':start')
   local_best=a0.copy(); local_best_sc=sc0
   n=len(a0)
   if time_limit_s < 3: return local_best
   # maxfun factor
   if n<=512: factor=500; cap=50000
   elif n<=2048: factor=200; cap=20000
   elif n<=8192: factor=60; cap=12000
   else: factor=30; cap=8000
   maxfun=int(min(cap, max(100, time_limit_s*factor)))
   maxiter=max(200, maxfun*3)
   gtol=1e-7 if beta > n else 1e-6
   cb=partial(object_func, beta=beta, lam=10.0) maybe object_func signature differs.
   res=minimize(cb, a0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=get_bounds(n), options={'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':gtol,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30,'disp':False})
   if getattr(res,'success',False) or hasattr(res,'x'):
       a_out=normalize(np.clip(res.x,0,1000))
       sc=exact_score(a_out)
       if sc < local_best_sc - 1e-13:
          local_best=a_out; local_best_sc=sc
          update_state(global_state,local_state,sc,a_out,tag+':lbfgs')
       else:
          # maybe res.x exact worse but LSE value better? keep local
          pass
   # Also try a tiny perturbation of res? no
   return local_best
```
But `object_func` for weighted has different parameters (idx, vals) not beta. We can write two separate run functions to avoid generic.

16. `def local_optimize_a(a0, deadline, global_state, local_state, rng, tag):`

def local_optimize_a(...):
   a=normalize(a0)
   sc=exact_score(a); update_state(...,tag+':input')

```

17. `def weighted_peak_optimize(a0, deadline, global_state, local_state, tag):`
```
   a=normalize(a0); sc=exact_score(a); update...
   n=len(a); passes=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 30 and passes<4:
      p=normalize(a); c=conv_real(p)
      M=c.size
      K=min(200, max(10, M//30)) # for 7000 K=200? M//30=464 -> cap200
      idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
      # maybe include max always
      vals=np.full(K,1.0/K)
      t_pass = min(90.0, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 20)/ max(1, 4-passes))
      if t_pass<15: break
      a_out=run_lbfgs_weighted(a, idx, vals, t_pass, global_state, local_state, tag+':w'+str(passes))
      sc_out=exact_score(a_out); sc_cur=exact_score(a)
      if sc_out < sc_cur - 1e-13:
         a=a_out
      else:
         break
      passes+=1
   return normalize(a)
```

18. `def iterative_local_search(a0, deadline, global_state, local_state, rng, tag):`
```
   a=normalize(a0); sc=exact_score(a); update...
   while time < deadline-60:
      scale=0.02+0.08*rng.random()
      mode=int(rng.integers(0,5))
      a_p=perturb_seq(a,rng,scale,mode)
      # choose beta: if n small base else n or 5n
      n=len(a); beta = float(n) if rng.random()<0.5 else 5*float(n)
      t_left=deadline-time-40
      alloc=min(90.0, t_left)
      a_out=run_lbfgs_lse(a_p,beta,alloc,global_state,local_state,tag+':ils')
      sc_out=exact_score(a_out); sc=exact_score(a)
      if sc_out < sc - 1e-13:
         a=a_out
      # Sometimes weighted small
      if time<deadline-60 and rng.random()<0.3:
         a=weighted_peak_optimize(a_p, min(time+30, deadline-20), ...)
         update
      if no improvement several times, reduce scale? Could track.
   return a
```

def coarse_search(t_end, rng, global_state):
   coarse_state={'score':inf,'a':None}
   m_list = [256,512,1024]
   # fractions maybe [0.3,0.4,0.3]
   time_cursor=time.monotonic()
   for m in m_list:
      if time_cursor >= t_end - 30: break
      alloc = min(90.0, (t_end - time_cursor)*0.45)
      if alloc<20: break
      sub_deadline = min(t_end, time.monotonic()+alloc)
      local_m={'score':inf,'a':None}
      inits=make_initial_seqs(m, rng, None, limit=5)
      # allocate per init
      for idx,init in enumerate(inits):
         left=sub_deadline-time.monotonic()
         if left<10: break
         per=left/(len(inits)-idx)
         a=local_optimize_a(init, min(sub_deadline, time.monotonic()+per), global_state, local_m, rng, f'coarse{m}_i{idx}')
      # LP refine
      if local_m['a'] is not None and time.monotonic() < sub_deadline-10:
         a_lp=lp_refine(local_m['a'], n_iter=2, mix=0.5, deadline=sub_deadline)
         if a_lp is not None:
            sc=exact_score(a_lp); update_state(global_state, local_m, sc, a_lp, f'coarse{m}_lp')
      if local_m['score'] < coarse_state['score'] - 1e-13:
         coarse_state['score']=local_m['score']; coarse_state['a']=local_m['a'].copy()
      time_cursor=time.monotonic()
   return coarse_state
```
But local_optimize_a for m=256 with betas 4 could be heavy? m small, factor 500, fast. Good.

`lp_refine`:
```
def lp_refine(f, n_iter=2, mix=0.5, deadline=time + many):
   best=f.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(best)
   for _ in range(n_iter):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      g=lp_update(best)
      if g is None: break
      mixed=normalize((1-mix)*best + mix*g)
      sc=exact_score(mixed)
      if sc < best_sc:
         best=mixed; best_sc=sc
   return best
```
`lp_update`:
```
def lp_update(f, deadline_extra=None):
   f=normalize(f); m=f.size
   if m<16 or m>2048: return None
   c=conv_real(f); M=max conv
   if M<=0: return None
   M *= 1.0001
   if lil_matrix is None: return None
   A=lil_matrix((2*m-1,m),dtype=float)
   for i,fi in enumerate(f):
      if fi>0:
         A[i:i+m, i] = fi
   A=A.tocsr()
   try:
      res=linprog(-np.ones(m), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*m-1,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*m, method='highs', options={'presolve':'high'})
   except Exception:
      try: res=linprog(-np.ones(m), A_ub=A.toarray(), ...)
      except: return None
   if res.status not in (0,4): return None? HiGHS status 0 optimal, 4 iteration limit maybe okay.
   g=np.clip(res.x,0,None); s=g.sum(); if s<1e-12: return None
   return g/s
```
Need if `linprog` with sparse not supported, fallback dense only m<=512. Use `try`.
LP may be slow for m=1024; okay. To avoid too long, we only call after local and before deadline. But linprog has no time limit. Could hang. To mitigate, set m=512 only? Or 768. Let's set `lp_update` only if m <= 768. Coarse m list [256,512,768]? Use 512 only for LP. For m=1024 skip. Use time guard not possible. I think 512 safe.

Let's define `run_lbfgs_lse` and `run_lbfgs_weighted` with optional `gtol`, `maxfun_factor`.

Code snippets:
```
def run_lbfgs_lse(a0, beta, time_limit_s, global_state, local_state, tag=''):
    a0 = normalize(a0)
    sc0 = exact_score(a0)
    update_state(global_state, local_state, sc0, a0, tag + ':init')
    local_best = a0.copy()
    local_best_sc = sc0
    n = len(a0)
    time_limit_s = max(1.0, float(time_limit_s))
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + time_limit_s > ... no, min handles in caller.
    if n <= 512: factor, cap = 500, 50000
    elif n <= 2048: 200,20000
    elif n <= 8192: 60,12000
    else: 30,8000
    maxfun = int(min(cap, max(120, time_limit_s * factor)))
    maxiter = int(min(200000, max(200, maxfun * 3)))
    gtol = 1e-7 if beta > 10*n else 1e-6
    cb = partial(lse_obj_a, beta=float(beta), lam=10.0)
    try:
       res = minimize(cb, a0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=get_bounds(n), options={...})
    except Exception as e:
       print(f'lbfgs error {e}')
       return local_best
    x = getattr(res,'x',None)
    if x is not None:
       x = np.asarray(x,float)
       x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=10.0, neginf=0.0)
       x = np.clip(x,0,10.0)
       if x.sum() > 1e-12:
          a_out = normalize(x)
          sc = exact_score(a_out)
          if sc < local_best_sc - 1e-13:
             local_best = a_out; local_best_sc=sc; update_state(...)
    return local_best
```
`exact_score` uses normalize, so if x sum not 1, okay.

`run_lbfgs_weighted` same with `weighted_obj_a` and gtol maybe 1e-7.

def local_optimize_a(a0, deadline, global_state, local_state, rng, tag=''):
    a = normalize(a0)
    sc = exact_score(a); update_state(...)
    n = len(a)
    betas = make_betas(n)
    # Maybe if local_state already has good same length, start from it instead of a0? For repeated calls same length, using current local best can be better. If a0 differs but worse, we still want to try a0. But for sequences of inits, if local_state has best, we can start from local_state after first init to continue refining. The function as called will optimize a0; after it, local_state best. Next init should perhaps start from a0 (new), not local_state, to explore. That's fine.
    for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
       now=time.monotonic(); if now>deadline-25: break
       t_left=deadline-now-15
       alloc=min(120.0, t_left/(len(betas)-idx))
       if alloc<10: alloc=min(10.0, t_left/2)
       a=run_lbfgs_lse(a,beta,alloc,global_state,local_state,tag+':b'+str(int(beta)))
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45 and rng.random()<0.7:
           a=perturb_seq(a,rng,scale=0.01+0.01*rng.random(),mode=int(rng.integers(0,5)))
           scp=exact_score(a); update_state(...)
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45:
       a=weighted_peak_optimize(a, min(deadline-15, time.monotonic()+90), global_state, local_state, tag+':w')
    return normalize(a)
```

Now, ILS uses `run_lbfgs_lse` directly, not local_optimize (to avoid multiple betas each trial). It can also use weighted for promising perturb.

```
def iterative_local_search(a0, deadline, global_state, local_state, rng, tag):
   a=normalize(a0); sc=exact; update; stagnant=0; scale=0.03
   n=len(a)
   while time < deadline - 60:
      a_p=perturb_seq(a,rng,scale,int(rng.integers(0,5)))
      beta = float(n) if rng.random()<0.5 else max(10.0,5*float(n))
      t_left=deadline-time-50
      alloc=min(70.0, t_left)
      a_out=run_lbfgs_lse(a_p,beta,alloc,global_state,local_state,tag)
      sc_out=exact_score(a_out); sc_cur=exact_score(a)
      if sc_out < sc_cur - 1e-13:
          a=a_out; stagnant=0
      else:
          stagnant += 1
          if stagnant >= 3:
             scale = min(0.25, scale*1.6); stagnant=0
          if scale > 0.2:
             scale = 0.03
      if time < deadline - 90 and rng.random() < 0.2:
          a_t = weighted_peak_optimize(a_p, min(deadline-30, time.monotonic()+40), global_state, local_state, tag+':w')
          sc_t=exact_score(a_t); if sc_t<exact_score(a): a=a_t
   return normalize(a)
```

```
def make_power(alpha,n,side=0):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    if side==1: x=1.0-x
    b=np.exp(-alpha*np.log(np.clip(x,1e-12,None)))
    # add small uniform to avoid zeros at right
    b=b + 0.05
    return normalize(b)
```

def explore_lengths(t_end, rng, global_state):
    base = global_state['a']
    if base is None: return
    n_base=len(base)
    candidates=[]
    for n_try in [8192, 10240, 12288, int(n_base*0.75), int(n_base*1.25)]:
       if 2048<=n_try<=12288 and n_try != n_base: candidates.append(int(n_try))
    # unique sorted
    candidates=sorted(set(candidates))
    if not candidates: return
    for i,n_try in enumerate(candidates):
       if time.monotonic() > t_end - 30: break
       left=t_end-time
       alloc=min(120.0, left/(len(candidates)-i))
       if alloc<20: break
       a_init=resample_seq(base,n_try)
       loc={'score':np.inf,'a':None}
       local_optimize_a(a_init, min(time+alloc, t_end-10), global_state, loc, rng, f'lexp{n_try}')
       base = global_state['a'] # maybe improved
```

def final_polish(a0, deadline, global_state):
    if a0 is None: return None
    loc={'score':global_state['score'], 'a':a0.copy()}
    a=normalize(a0)
    update_state(global_state,loc, exact_score(a),a,'final_init')
    n=len(a)
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-20:
       beta=max(100.0, 5.0*float(n))
       a=run_lbfgs_lse(a,beta, min(45, deadline-time-10), global_state, loc, 'final_lse')
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-15:
       a=weighted_peak_optimize(a, deadline-5, global_state, loc, 'final_w')
    return normalize(a)
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
with a focus on fast correct FFT-based gradients, local L-BFGS polishing,
weighted active-peak refinement, coarse-to-fine shaping, occasional small LP
updates, iterated perturbations, and length exploration.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from functools import partial

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.sparse import lil_matrix
except Exception:
    lil_matrix = None


_BOUNDS_CACHE = {}


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def conv_real(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:m]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c


def cross_corr(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    mw = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or mw == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(n, 0), dtype=np.float64)
    L = n + mw - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    ar = a[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    p = normalize(a)
    c = conv_real(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * p.size * mx)


def update_state(global_state, local_state, sc, a, tag=""):
    sc = float(sc)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return False
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return False
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return False
    a = a / s
    if sc < float(local_state.get("score", np.inf)) - 1e-13:
        local_state["score"] = sc
        local_state["a"] = a.copy()
        print(f"  {tag}: {sc:.10f} n={a.size}")
        if sc < float(global_state.get("score", np.inf)) - 1e-13:
            global_state["score"] = sc
            global_state["a"] = a.copy()
        return True
    return False


def state_best(local_state, fallback):
    fb = normalize(fallback)
    a = local_state.get("a", None)
    if a is None or len(a) != len(fb):
        return fb
    return normalize(a)


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is None:
            return None
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size >= 16 and arr.sum() > 1e-8:
            return normalize(arr)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def resample_seq(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    if a.size == n:
        return normalize(a)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(xn, xo, a)
    return normalize(b)


def make_power(alpha, n, side=0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    if side == 1:
        x = 1.0 - x
    b = np.exp(-float(alpha) * np.log(np.clip(x, 1e-12, None)))
    b = b + 1e-8
    return normalize(b)


def make_initial_seqs(n, rng, parent=None, limit=8):
    out = []
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if parent is not None:
        out.append(resample_seq(parent, n))
    out.append(normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    for alpha in (0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.49):
        out.append(make_power(alpha, n, 0))
        out.append(make_power(alpha, n, 1))
    for lam in (-1.0, 1.0):
        out.append(normalize(np.exp(float(lam) * (2.0 * x - 1.0))))
    out.append(normalize(np.maximum(0.05, np.cos(np.pi * x) ** 2)))
    out.append(normalize(np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.1))
    if limit is not None:
        out = out[:limit]
    return out


def perturb_seq(a, rng, scale=0.03, mode=0):
    a = normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    mode = int(mode) % 5
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    if mode == 0:
        b = a * (1.0 + float(scale) * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        freq = 1.0 + 12.0 * rng.random()
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
        b = a * (1.0 + float(scale) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase))
    elif mode == 2:
        r = rng.standard_normal(n)
        asym = r - r[::-1]
        b = a + 0.5 * float(scale) * asym
    elif mode == 3:
        b = a.copy()
        k = max(1, n // 10)
        sel = rng.choice(n, size=min(k, n), replace=False)
        f = 1.0 + 2.0 * float(scale) * (rng.random(sel.size) - 0.5)
        b[sel] *= f
    else:
        b = a * (1.0 + 2.0 * float(scale) * (rng.random(n) - 0.5))
    return normalize(np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0))


def get_bounds(n):
    n = int(n)
    b = _BOUNDS_CACHE.get(n, None)
    if b is None:
        b = [(0.0, 10.0) for _ in range(n)]
        _BOUNDS_CACHE[n] = b
    return b


def lse_obj_a(a, beta, lam=5.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=10.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 10.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return 1e6, -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = a / S
    c = conv_real(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        L = float(np.mean(c))
    else:
        diff = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
        diff = np.clip(diff, -745.0, 0.0)
        z = np.exp(diff)
        Z = float(z.sum())
        if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
            w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            L = cmax
        else:
            w = z / Z
            L = cmax + float(np.log(Z)) / float(beta)
    R = cross_corr(p, w)
    D = 2.0 * R
    alpha = 2.0 * float(n)
    g = (alpha / S) * (D - 2.0 * L)
    g = g + 2.0 * float(lam) * (S - 1.0)
    val = alpha * L + float(lam) * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e6
    if not np.isfinite(g).all():
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), g


def weighted_obj_a(a, idx, vals, lam=5.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=10.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 10.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return 1e6, -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = a / S
    c = conv_real(p)
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=int)
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    if idx.size == 0:
        J = 0.0
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        J = float(np.dot(c[idx], vals))
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = vals
    R = cross_corr(p, w)
    D = 2.0 * R
    alpha = 2.0 * float(n)
    g = (alpha / S) * (D - 2.0 * J)
    g = g + 2.0 * float(lam) * (S - 1.0)
    val = alpha * J + float(lam) * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e6
    if not np.isfinite(g).all():
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), g


def make_betas(n):
    b = float(max(1, int(n)))
    return [max(10.0, b / 4.0), max(20.0, b), max(50.0, 5.0 * b), max(100.0, 20.0 * b)]


def run_lbfgs_lse(a0, beta, time_limit_s, global_state, local_state, tag=""):
    a0 = normalize(a0)
    sc0 = exact_score(a0)
    update_state(global_state, local_state, sc0, a0, tag + ":init")
    local_best = a0.copy()
    local_best_sc = sc0
    n = int(a0.size)
    time_limit_s = max(1.0, float(time_limit_s))

    if n <= 512:
        factor, cap = 500, 50000
    elif n <= 2048:
        factor, cap = 200, 20000
    elif n <= 8192:
        factor, cap = 60, 12000
    else:
        factor, cap = 30, 8000

    maxfun = int(min(cap, max(120, time_limit_s * factor)))
    maxiter = int(min(200000, max(200, 3 * maxfun)))
    gtol = 1e-7 if float(beta) > 10.0 * n else 1e-6

    cb = partial(lse_obj_a, beta=float(beta), lam=5.0)
    try:
        res = minimize(
            cb, a0, jac=True, method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=get_bounds(n),
            options={
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": gtol,
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "maxls": 30,
                "disp": False,
            }
        )
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"    lse L-BFGS exception: {e}")
        return state_best(local_state, local_best)

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is not None:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=10.0, neginf=0.0)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 10.0)
        if x.sum() > 1e-12:
            a_out = normalize(x)
            sc = exact_score(a_out)
            if sc < local_best_sc - 1e-13:
                local_best = a_out
                local_best_sc = sc
                update_state(global_state, local_state, sc, a_out, tag + ":lbfgs")
    return state_best(local_state, local_best)


def run_lbfgs_weighted(a0, idx, vals, time_limit_s, global_state, local_state, tag=""):
    a0 = normalize(a0)
    sc0 = exact_score(a0)
    update_state(global_state, local_state, sc0, a0, tag + ":init")
    local_best = a0.copy()
    local_best_sc = sc0
    n = int(a0.size)
    time_limit_s = max(1.0, float(time_limit_s))

    if n <= 512:
        factor, cap = 400, 40000
    elif n <= 2048:
        factor, cap = 160, 18000
    elif n <= 8192:
        factor, cap = 50, 10000
    else:
        factor, cap = 25, 7000

    maxfun = int(min(cap, max(100, time_limit_s * factor)))
    maxiter = int(min(200000, max(200, 3 * maxfun)))

    cb = partial(weighted_obj_a, idx=np.asarray(idx, dtype=int), vals=np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64), lam=5.0)
    try:
        res = minimize(
            cb, a0, jac=True, method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=get_bounds(n),
            options={
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "maxls": 30,
                "disp": False,
            }
        )
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"    weighted L-BFGS exception: {e}")
        return state_best(local_state, local_best)

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is not None:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=10.0, neginf=0.0)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 10.0)
        if x.sum() > 1e-12:
            a_out = normalize(x)
            sc = exact_score(a_out)
            if sc < local_best_sc - 1e-13:
                local_best = a_out
                local_best_sc = sc
                update_state(global_state, local_state, sc, a_out, tag + ":weighted")
    return state_best(local_state, local_best)


def weighted_peak_optimize(a0, deadline, global_state, local_state, tag=""):
    a = normalize(a0)
    sc = exact_score(a)
    update_state(global_state, local_state, sc, a, tag + ":init")
    a = state_best(local_state, a)

    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    passes = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 35 and passes < 4:
        p = normalize(a)
        c = conv_real(p)
        M = int(c.size)
        K = int(min(120, max(8, M // 40)))
        if M <= K:
            K = M
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        vals = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)

        left = deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0
        t_pass = min(90.0, left / max(1, 4 - passes))
        if t_pass < 15.0:
            break
        a_out = run_lbfgs_weighted(a, idx, vals, t_pass, global_state, local_state, tag + f":pass{passes}")
        sc_out = exact_score(a_out)
        if sc_out < best_sc - 1e-13:
            a = a_out
            best_sc = sc_out
            passes += 1
        else:
            break
    return normalize(a)


def local_optimize_a(a0, deadline, global_state, local_state, rng, tag=""):
    a = normalize(a0)
    sc = exact_score(a)
    update_state(global_state, local_state, sc, a, tag + ":input")
    a = state_best(local_state, a)
    n = len(a)
    betas = make_betas(n)
    remaining = len(betas)

    for beta in betas:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 25.0:
            break
        t_left = deadline - now - 15.0
        alloc = min(150.0, t_left / max(1, remaining))
        if alloc < 12.0:
            alloc = min(12.0, max(0.0, t_left / 2.0))
        if alloc <= 1.0:
            break
        a = run_lbfgs_lse(a, beta, alloc, global_state, local_state, tag + f":beta{int(beta)}")
        a = state_best(local_state, a)
        remaining -= 1

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0 and rng.random() < 0.65:
            scale = 0.008 + 0.02 * rng.random()
            mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
            a = perturb_seq(a, rng, scale=scale, mode=mode)
            update_state(global_state, local_state, exact_score(a), a, tag + ":perturb")
            a = state_best(local_state, a)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 55.0:
        w_end = min(deadline - 15.0, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
        a = weighted_peak_optimize(a, w_end, global_state, local_state, tag + ":weighted")
    return normalize(a)


def lp_update(f, max_m=768):
    if linprog is None or lil_matrix is None:
        return None
    f = normalize(f)
    m = int(f.size)
    if m < 16 or m > max_m:
        return None
    c = conv_real(f)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None
    M *= 1.0005
    A = lil_matrix((2 * m - 1, m), dtype=np.float64)
    for i, fi in enumerate(f):
        fi = float(fi)
        if fi > 0.0:
            A[i:i + m, i] = fi
    A = A.tocsr()
    b_ub = np.full(2 * m - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * m
    cobj = -np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs",
                      options={"presolve": "high"})
    except Exception:
        res = None
    if res is None and m <= 512:
        try:
            Ad = A.toarray()
            res = linprog(cobj, A_ub=Ad, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
        except Exception:
            res = None
    if res is None or getattr(res, "status", None) not in (0, 4):
        return None
    g = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if g is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return g / s


def lp_refine(f, n_iter=2, mix=0.5, deadline=None):
    best = normalize(f)
    best_sc = exact_score(best)
    for _ in range(int(max(0, n_iter))):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        g = lp_update(best)
        if g is None:
            break
        mixed = normalize((1.0 - float(mix)) * best + float(mix) * g)
        sc = exact_score(mixed)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best = mixed
            best_sc = sc
    return best


def coarse_search(t_end, rng, global_state):
    coarse_state = {"score": np.inf, "a": None}
    m_list = [256, 512, 1024]
    for idx, m in enumerate(m_list):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_end - 30.0:
            break
        left = t_end - now - 10.0
        alloc = min(100.0, left / max(1, len(m_list) - idx))
        if alloc < 20.0:
            break
        sub_deadline = min(t_end, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        local_m = {"score": np.inf, "a": None}
        inits = make_initial_seqs(m, rng, None, limit=5)
        for j, init in enumerate(inits):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= sub_deadline - 8.0:
                break
            t_left = sub_deadline - now - 5.0
            per = t_left / max(1, len(inits) - j)
            if per < 6.0:
                break
            local_optimize_a(init, min(sub_deadline, now + per), global_state, local_m, rng, f"coarse{m}_i{j}")
        if local_m.get("a") is not None and time.monotonic() < sub_deadline - 8.0:
            a_lp = lp_refine(local_m["a"], n_iter=2, mix=0.5, deadline=max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, sub_deadline - 2.0))
            if a_lp is not None:
                update_state(global_state, local_m, exact_score(a_lp), a_lp, f"coarse{m}_lp")
        if local_m.get("score", np.inf) < coarse_state["score"] - 1e-13:
            coarse_state["score"] = local_m["score"]
            coarse_state["a"] = local_m["a"].copy()
    return coarse_state


def iterative_local_search(a0, deadline, global_state, local_state, rng, tag="ils"):
    a = normalize(a0)
    sc = exact_score(a)
    update_state(global_state, local_state, sc, a, tag + ":input")
    a = state_best(local_state, a)
    n = len(a)
    scale = 0.03
    stagnant = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 55.0:
        a = state_best(local_state, a)
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
        a_p = perturb_seq(a, rng, scale=scale, mode=mode)
        beta = float(n) if rng.random() < 0.5 else max(50.0, 5.0 * float(n))
        left = deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0
        alloc = min(75.0, left)
        if alloc < 18.0:
            break
        a_out = run_lbfgs_lse(a_p, beta, alloc, global_state, local_state, tag + f":b{int(beta)}")
        sc_out = exact_score(a_out)
        sc_cur = exact_score(a)
        if sc_out < sc_cur - 1e-13:
            a = a_out
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant >= 3:
                scale = min(0.25, scale * 1.6)
                stagnant = 0
            if scale > 0.22:
                scale = 0.03

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 85.0 and rng.random() < 0.25:
            w_end = min(deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + 35.0)
            a_t = weighted_peak_optimize(a_p, w_end, global_state, local_state, tag + ":w")
            sc_t = exact_score(a_t)
            if sc_t < exact_score(a) - 1e-13:
                a = a_t
    return normalize(a)


def explore_lengths(t_end, rng, global_state):
    base = global_state.get("a", None)
    if base is None:
        return
    base = normalize(base)
    nb = int(base.size)
    cand = set()
    for nn in (4096, 8192, 10240, 12288, int(nb * 0.75), int(nb * 1.25)):
        if 2048 <= nn <= 12288 and nn != nb:
            cand.add(int(nn))
    cand = sorted(cand)
    if not cand:
        return
    for i, nn in enumerate(cand):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > t_end - 35.0:
            break
        left = t_end - now - 15.0
        alloc = min(120.0, left / max(1, len(cand) - i))
        if alloc < 25.0:
            break
        base = global_state.get("a", base)
        a_init = resample_seq(base, nn)
        loc = {"score": np.inf, "a": None}
        local_optimize_a(a_init, min(t_end, now + alloc), global_state, loc, rng, f"len{nn}")


def final_polish(a0, deadline, global_state):
    a = normalize(a0)
    loc = {"score": float(global_state.get("score", np.inf)), "a": a.copy()}
    update_state(global_state, loc, exact_score(a), a, "final:init")
    a = state_best(loc, a)
    n = len(a)
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        beta = max(200.0, 5.0 * float(n))
        t = min(50.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 12.0)
        if t > 12.0:
            a = run_lbfgs_lse(a, beta, t, global_state, loc, "final:lse")
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
        a = weighted_peak_optimize(a, deadline - 5.0, global_state, loc, "final:w")
    return normalize(a)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    try:
        bud = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        bud = 2400.0
    bud = min(2400.0, max(60.0, bud))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + bud - 30.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    global_state = {"score": np.inf, "a": None}
    parent = get_parent_sequence()

    if parent is not None:
        update_state(global_state, global_state, exact_score(parent), parent, "parent")
    if global_state["a"] is None:
        scratch = make_initial_seqs(2048, rng, None, limit=1)
        if scratch:
            update_state(global_state, global_state, exact_score(scratch[0]), scratch[0], "scratch")

    if global_state["a"] is None:
        out = [0.5] * 128
        return out

    T = max(60.0, deadline - t0)
    t_coarse_end = t0 + min(160.0, 0.07 * T) if T > 250.0 else t0 + 0.10 * T
    t_main_end = t0 + 0.45 * T
    t_ilss_end = t0 + 0.75 * T
    t_len_end = t0 + 0.93 * T

    coarse_state = {"score": np.inf, "a": None}
    if t_coarse_end > t0 + 45.0:
        coarse_state = coarse_search(t_coarse_end, rng, global_state)

    if parent is not None and 1024 <= len(parent) <= 14000:
        n_main = len(parent)
    else:
        n_main = 8192
    n_main = int(min(12288, max(2048, n_main)))

    main_inits = []
    if parent is not None:
        main_inits.append(resample_seq(parent, n_main))
    if coarse_state.get("a") is not None:
        main_inits.append(resample_seq(coarse_state["a"], n_main))
    if global_state.get("a") is not None and len(global_state["a"]) != n_main:
        main_inits.append(resample_seq(global_state["a"], n_main))
    main_inits.append(make_power(0.49, n_main, 0))
    main_inits.append(make_power(0.49, n_main, 1))
    main_inits.append(normalize(np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n_main)) + 0.1))

    st_main = {"score": np.inf, "a": None}
    if global_state.get("a") is not None and len(global_state["a"]) == n_main:
        st_main["score"] = global_state["score"]
        st_main["a"] = global_state["a"].copy()

    init_cursor = time.monotonic()
    for i, init in enumerate(main_inits):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > t_main_end - 30.0:
            break
        left = t_main_end - now - 15.0
        if left < 25.0:
            break
        if i == 0:
            alloc = min(420.0, left * 0.45)
        else:
            alloc = min(180.0, left / max(1, len(main_inits) - i))
        if alloc < 20.0:
            break
        local_optimize_a(init, min(t_main_end, now + alloc), global_state, st_main, rng, f"main{i}")

    base_a = global_state.get("a", None)
    if base_a is None:
        base_a = normalize(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0)
    st_base = {
        "score": float(global_state.get("score", np.inf)),
        "a": normalize(base_a).copy(),
    }
    if time.monotonic() < t_ilss_end - 30.0:
        iterative_local_search(base_a, t_ilss_end, global_state, st_base, rng, "ils")

    if time.monotonic() < t_len_end - 30.0:
        explore_lengths(t_len_end, rng, global_state)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        base_a = global_state.get("a", base_a)
        final_a = final_polish(base_a, deadline - 5.0, global_state)
        if final_a is not None:
            sc = exact_score(final_a)
            update_state(global_state, global_state, sc, final_a, "final")

    best_a = global_state.get("a", None)
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = normalize(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0)
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.nan_to_num(best_a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        best_a = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5

    out = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    internal = exact_score(best_a)
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        print(f"FINAL internal={internal:.10f} verified={verified:.10f} n={len(out)} time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"FINAL internal={internal:.10f} verification skipped: {e} n={len(out)} time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    return out
```
